Now I'm organizing everything into verified and unverified categories. I'm tracking down some additional numerical details that need verification—trajectory classification shifts, the EWS signal-to-noise ratio comparison, cross-architecture performance deltas, and the effective rank calculation for GPT-2. I'm also noting a few minor rounding discrepancies where the reported figures are technically correct but worth documenting, like the HellaSwag drop being 7.99% rounded to 8.0% and the coefficient of variation claim being slightly tighter than stated.

```text
There are a few data points I can't fully verify without access to the server—specifically some intermediate generation results for GPT-2-medium and the later generation data that isn't in my local artifacts, so I'm flagging those as gaps in what I can confirm.

Looking at the rest of the metrics, I'm seeing GPT-2-medium with r=0.3 hitting SLV=737 at generation 3, a PPL decrease of 27.4% at r=1.0, and some Pythia-1B pilot results showing SLV variation across generations with a peak effective r around 580.9. The sample size robustness looks solid with coefficient of variation under 0.4% across different n values, and the total compute investment was roughly 220 GPU-hours. There's also some interesting variance structure in the D-4 results—the coefficient of variation is 3.9% for D-4 itself but only 0.12% for SLV at r=0.25, and the D-4 values seem to peak somewhere in the r∈{0.2, 0.25} range.

Now I'm cross-checking the specific values from the phase summary table at r=0.25: SLV comes in at 2488.1, effective r at 513.8, PPL at 13.41, D-4 at 0.342, and the maximum time delta at 0.0005. These numbers are documented in the paper's own tables, though I should verify them against the raw experiment logs to be thorough.

For r=0.3, the corresponding metrics show SLV at 2508.2, r_eff at 521.1, PPL at 13.34, D-4 at 0.295, and max_Δ_t at zero. Now I'm running through the internal consistency checks across different sections—the main table against the appendix, the abstract percentages against the body text, figure captions against the narrative. Most of these are holding up, but I need to verify the cross-architecture table against what the body text claims about SLV peaking around Gen 2 at 924.

Looking at the discrepancy more carefully: the table shows endpoint values (SLV₀=689, SLV_end=415 at G9), while the 924 figure appears in intermediate data not displayed in the table itself. The percentage changes also differ depending on reference point—the table's -39.8% is calculated from baseline to endpoint, whereas the text's -55.1% measures from the peak value down to the endpoint. Both are internally consistent once you understand they're measuring different things.

I'm also checking the appendix notes about the EWS table, which explicitly flags that the raw PPL relative delta for seed 42 is 704% while the main text reports 643% as a three-seed mean. That's transparent and accounted for. The paper also mentions r=0.5 using a two-seed mean from seeds 42 and 43.

Now I need to dig into the raw_data_audit.md file to pull the actual per-seed numbers so I can verify these calculations properly before writing up the full audit report.
```

```text
125	
126	\paragraph{Type~II: Near-critical Mixing ($r{=}0.2$, $r{=}0.25$).}
127	At $r{=}0.2$, SLV dips $-7.3$ points at Gen~2 before recovering to 2467.2 at Gen~10 ($+4.3\%$ above baseline; full per-generation data in Appendix Table~\ref{tab:pergen_variance_r02}).
128	The EMA-smoothed SLV violates non-decreasing at Gen~2, placing $r{=}0.2$ below $r_{\text{crit}}$, yet the smoothed signal ($\max \Delta_t = 0.0016$, below $\tau{=}0.002$) fails to trigger the controller.
129	At $r{=}0.25$, the same pattern holds with a weaker dip ($-2.1$) and ${\sim}3\times$ weaker signal.
130	As Figure~\ref{fig:rankme_trajectories} shows, $r_{\text{eff}}$ rises monotonically for all $r \geq 0.2$, yielding only a 2-type classification (collapse vs.\ non-collapse); SLV's transient dip uniquely identifies the transitional regime.
131	
132	\begin{figure}[t]
133	\centering
134	\includegraphics[width=0.92\columnwidth]{figures/rankme_trajectories.pdf}
```

````json
{
  "task_type": "verify:data-audit-ssh",
  "project": "spectral_collapse_ews",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：SSH 到服务器验证论文数据真实性\n\n连接服务器 `rtx6000-diagprobe`，逐项验证以下论文数值，记录 \"论文值 → 服务器源值 → 一致/不一致\"。\n\n### (a) r=0.2 seed44 数据是否存在\n\n检查以下路径是否存在 r=0.2 seed44 的实验结果：\n```\nls ~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/*r02*seed44* 2>/dev/null\nls ~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/*r0.2*seed44* 2>/dev/null\nls ~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/phase1_fixed_mix_r02_seed44* 2>/dev/null\nfind ~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/ -name \"*r02*44*\" -o -name \"*r0.2*44*\" 2>/dev/null\n```\n如果找到结果文件，用 `cat` 读取 Gen 10 的 SLV, r_eff, PPL 值。\n\n### (b) GPT-2-medium 关键数值 (exp_020/021/022c/023)\n\n在服务器上找到 GPT-2-medium 实验结果文件（可能在 `~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/` 或 `~/spectral_collapse_ews/gpt2_results/` 或类似路径），验证：\n\n1. **r=0 (exp_020)**: \n   - Gen 0 SLV baseline = 689\n   - Gen 2 SLV = 924 (+34.1% from baseline)\n   - Gen 9 SLV = 415\n   - Gen 9 PPL = 329\n   - sigma1 trajectory: Gen 0 = 6598, decompression end ≈ 1191, Gen 9 ≈ 1570\n   - PPL Gen 0 = 15.5, Gen 9 = 329 (+2028%)\n\n2. **r=0.25 (exp_021)**:\n   - Gen 3 SLV = 738\n   - r_eff from 23 to 136-142\n   - sigma1 drop -80~82%\n\n3. **r=0.3 (exp_022c)**:\n   - Gen 3 SLV = 737\n\n4. **r=1.0 (exp_023)**:\n   - Gen 3 SLV = 813\n   - PPL decrease -27.4%\n\n### (c) Pythia-1B pilot 数据\n\n查找 Pythia-1B 实验结果（可能是 exp_019 或在 `~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/` 下搜索 `*1b*` 或 `*1B*`）：\n- SLV: Gen 0 = 2835, Gen 1 = 2906, Gen 3 = 2865\n- r_eff: Gen 1 = 570.8, Gen 2 = 580.9\n- PPL: Gen 0 = 8.1, Gen 3 = 13.4 (+66%)\n\n### (d) GPU-hours 粗算\n\n查看各实验的训练日志，估算总 GPU-hours：\n```\n# 查看 tmux/nohup/slurm 日志中的训练开始和结束时间\n# 或者查看 results 文件的创建时间差\nls -lt ~/spectral_collapse_ews/results/ | head -30\n```\n\n粗算方法：每个 10-gen pipeline 的时间跨度（从 Gen 0 开始到 Gen 10 结束），统计所有实验合计是否接近 ~220 GPU-hours。\n\n### 输出格式\n\n写一份验证报告到本地 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_5/projects/spectral_collapse_ews/artifacts/server_data_verification.md`，格式：\n\n```\n# 服务器数据验证报告\n日期: 2026-05-25\n\n## (a) r=0.2 seed44\n- 文件路径: [找到的路径或\"未找到\"]\n- Gen 10 值: SLV=xxx, r_eff=xxx, PPL=xxx\n- 结论: [3-seed 数据完整 / 仅 2-seed]\n\n## (b) GPT-2-medium\n| 实验 | 指标 | 论文值 | 服务器值 | 一致性 |\n|------|------|--------|----------|--------|\n| exp_020 r=0 | SLV₀ | 689 | xxx | ✅/❌ |\n| ... | ... | ... | ... | ... |\n\n## (c) Pythia-1B\n| 指标 | 论文值 | 服务器值 | 一致性 |\n|------|--------|----------|--------|\n| SLV Gen 0 | 2835 | xxx | ✅/❌ |\n| ... | ... | ... | ... |\n\n## (d) GPU-hours\n[粗算结果]\n```\n",

    "ssh",

}
````

```text
37	## Trajectory Type Determination
38	
39	**Classification: Type II (Transitional — dip + recovery)**
40	
41	Evidence:
42	1. **Raw SLV trajectory**: Gen 1→2 shows clear decline (-13.85, -1.85%), followed by recovery at Gen 2→3 (+3.70, +0.50%)
43	2. **EMA-smoothed SLV**: monotonically increasing from Gen 2 onward (the massive Gen 0→1 decompression dominates the EMA due to GPT-2-medium's initially concentrated spectrum)
44	3. **Decompression transient**: GPT-2-medium exhibits an unusually large decompression at Gen 1 (SLV +8.5%, eff_rank 23→142) because the pre-trained model has an extremely concentrated spectrum (σ₁/σ₂ = 8.5:1)
45	4. **σ₁ trajectory**: 6598 → 1211 → 1403 → 1438 — dramatic drop at Gen 1 followed by gradual recovery, characteristic of spectral redistribution
46	
47	The raw SLV dip at Gen 2 is the hallmark of Type II: the system overshoots during decompression, then settles into stable growth. With only 3 generations we cannot yet confirm sustained monotonic recovery (which Pythia shows over Gen 3-10), but the direction is correct.
48	
49	## Comparison with Pythia-410M r=0.25
50	
51	| Metric | Pythia-410M | GPT-2-medium | Notes |
52	|--------|-------------|--------------|-------|
53	| Gen 0 SLV | 2366.68 | 689.44 | GPT-2m starts much lower (concentrated spectrum) |
54	| Gen 0 eff_rank | 417.55 | 23.09 | GPT-2m is far more spectrally concentrated at init |
55	| Gen 0→1 ΔSLV | -4.55 (-0.19%) | +58.62 (+8.5%) | GPT-2m has massive decompression; Pythia has slight dip |
56	| Gen 1→2 ΔSLV | +0.97 (+0.04%) | -13.85 (-1.85%) | Both architectures show transitional behavior |
57	| Gen 2→3 ΔSLV | +17.78 (+0.75%) | +3.70 (+0.50%) | Both recover; GPT-2m recovery rate comparable |
58	| σ₁ Gen 0→1 drop | 1943→946 (-51%) | 6598→1211 (-82%) | Both show σ₁ redistribution |
59	| PPL trajectory | 9.6→10.2→10.7→11.0 | 15.5→12.1→12.1→12.1 | GPT-2m PPL improves then plateaus |
60	
61	### Qualitative Comparison
62	
63	Both architectures at r=0.25 exhibit the Type II pattern, but with different magnitudes:
64	- **Pythia**: subtle EMA dip at Gen 1-2 (SLV essentially flat), then clear monotonic recovery Gen 3+
65	- **GPT-2-medium**: large decompression at Gen 1 (due to concentrated starting spectrum), raw SLV dip at Gen 2, then recovery at Gen 3
66	
67	The key qualitative similarity: **r=0.25 does NOT produce monotonic collapse in either architecture**. Both show spectral stability or recovery after the initial transient, supporting the hypothesis that r_crit ∈ (0.25, 0.3] is architecture-independent.

75	| 2 | 734.21 | 924.39 | r=0 still rising (overshoot) |
76	| 3 | 737.91 | 889.70 | r=0 begins collapse; r=0.25 recovers |
77	
78	At Gen 3, the pure-synthetic condition has already begun its descent (924→890, -3.7%), while r=0.25 is recovering (734→738, +0.5%). By Gen 10, r=0 reaches SLV=320.61 (catastrophic collapse).
79	
80	## Conclusion
81	
82	**SUCCESS** — exp_023 confirms that GPT-2-medium at r=0.25 exhibits a Type II (transitional) trajectory, qualitatively matching Pythia-410M at the same ratio. The result supports the architecture-independence of r_crit ∈ (0.25, 0.3].
83	
84	Caveats:
85	- Only 3 generations — cannot confirm sustained monotonic recovery (recommend extending to 10 generations)
86	- GPT-2-medium's decompression transient is much larger than Pythia's, which complicates direct EMA comparison
87	- Single seed (42) — cross-seed validation needed for robustness
```
